2 Corinthians 8
Q1. Multiple choice: What does the appeal to Christ's generosity contribute to the collection for others? (8:7-15) Hard
A) The aim is to enrich a few while ignoring need. B) Every donation guarantees financial profit. C) Willingness and actual ability are declared irrelevant. D) Generous care is shaped by Christ's self-giving and concern that needs be met.
Answer: D. Generous care is shaped by Christ's self-giving and concern that needs be met. Paul discusses willingness, ability and a fair sharing of provision. This is not a formula by which a donation purchases personal riches.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… who puts the same earnest care for you into the heart of ______.” (8:16); “… with us in this grace, which is served by us to the ______ of the Lord himself, and to show our readiness.” (8:19, WEB) Hard
A) Titus; faithfulness B) glory; Titus C) Titus; glory D) Matthias; glory
Answer: C. Titus; glory. Verse 16 reads “… who puts the same earnest care for you into the heart of Titus.”; verse 19 reads “… with us in this grace, which is served by us to the glory of the Lord himself, and to show our readiness.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… their lack, that their abundance also may become a supply for your lack; that there may be equality.” (8:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… their lack, that their abundance also may become a supply for your lack; that there may be equality.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… be willing, so there may be the completion also out of your ______.”? (8:11, WEB) Medium
A) accord B) proving C) indeed D) ability
Answer: D. ability. The passage specifies ability: “… be willing, so there may be the completion also out of your ability.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… abound in everything, in faith, utterance, knowledge, all earnestness, and in your ______ to us, see that you also abound in this grace.”? (8:7, WEB) Easy
A) honor B) shame C) love D) wisdom
Answer: C. love. 2 Corinthians 8:7 uses love: “… abound in everything, in faith, utterance, knowledge, all earnestness, and in your love to us, see that you also abound in this grace.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… the grace of our Lord Jesus ______, that, though he was rich, yet for your sakes he became ______, that you through his poverty might become …” (8:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: Christ; poor. The wording is “… the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ, that, though he was rich, yet for your sakes he became poor, that you through his poverty might become …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “begging us with much entreaty to receive this ______ and the fellowship in the service to the saints.” (8:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: grace. The cited words are “begging us with much entreaty to receive this grace and the fellowship in the service to the saints.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “how that in much proof of affliction the abundance of their ______ and their deep poverty abounded to the riches of their liberality.” (8:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: joy. The verse reads, “how that in much proof of affliction the abundance of their joy and their deep poverty abounded to the riches of their liberality.”
2 Corinthians 9
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… supplication on your behalf, yearn for you by reason of the exceeding ______ of God in you.” (9:14); “Now thanks be to God for his ______ gift!” (9:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: grace; unspeakable. The first passage reads “… supplication on your behalf, yearn for you by reason of the exceeding grace of God in you.”; the second reads “Now thanks be to God for his unspeakable gift!”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… for lack among the saints, but abounds also through many givings of ______ to God;” (9:12); “… service, they glorify God for the obedience of your confession to the ______ of Christ, and for the liberality of your contribution to them and to …” (9:13, WEB) Hard
A) Gospel; thanks B) thanks; Macedonia C) thanks; Gospel D) loves; Gospel
Answer: C. thanks; Gospel. Verse 12 reads “… for lack among the saints, but abounds also through many givings of thanks to God;”; verse 13 reads “… service, they glorify God for the obedience of your confession to the Gospel of Christ, and for the liberality of your contribution to them and to …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Let each man give according as he has determined in his ______; not grudgingly, or under compulsion; for God loves a cheerful giver.”? (9:7, WEB) Medium
A) blood B) heart C) tongue D) bones
Answer: B. heart. The passage specifies heart: “Let each man give according as he has determined in his heart; not grudgingly, or under compulsion; for God loves a cheerful giver.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… your readiness, of which I boast on your behalf to them of ______, that Achaia has been prepared for a year past. Your zeal has stirred …” (9:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Macedonia. The verse reads, “… your readiness, of which I boast on your behalf to them of Macedonia, that Achaia has been prepared for a year past. Your zeal has stirred …”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… unprepared, we (to say nothing of you) should be disappointed in this ______ boasting.” (9:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: confident. The cited words are “… unprepared, we (to say nothing of you) should be disappointed in this confident boasting.”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… and multiply your seed for sowing, and increase the fruits of your joy;” (9:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… and multiply your seed for sowing, and increase the fruits of your righteousness;” It says righteousness, not joy.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “I thought it necessary therefore to entreat the ______ that they would go before to you, and arrange ahead of time the generous gift that you …”? (9:5, WEB) Easy
A) husband B) wife C) brothers D) husbands
Answer: C. brothers. 2 Corinthians 9:5 uses brothers: “I thought it necessary therefore to entreat the brothers that they would go before to you, and arrange ahead of time the generous gift that you …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Remember this: he who sows ______ will also reap sparingly. He who sows bountifully will also reap ______.” (9:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: sparingly; bountifully. The wording is “Remember this: he who sows sparingly will also reap sparingly. He who sows bountifully will also reap bountifully.” The missing terms belong in that order.
2 Corinthians 10
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “But we will not boast beyond proper limits, but within the ______ with which God appointed to us, which reach even to you.” (10:13); “… proper limits in other men's labors, but having hope that as your ______ grows, we will be abundantly enlarged by you in our sphere of influence,” (10:15, WEB) Hard
A) men's; faith B) boundaries; faith C) faith; boundaries D) boundaries; someone
Answer: B. boundaries; faith. Verse 13 reads “But we will not boast beyond proper limits, but within the boundaries with which God appointed to us, which reach even to you.”; verse 15 reads “… proper limits in other men's labors, but having hope that as your faith grows, we will be abundantly enlarged by you in our sphere of influence,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Now I ______, myself, entreat you by the humility and gentleness of Christ; I who in your presence am lowly among you, but being absent am …” (10:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Paul. The verse reads, “Now I Paul, myself, entreat you by the humility and gentleness of Christ; I who in your presence am lowly among you, but being absent am …”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “For, "His letters," they say, "are weighty and strong, but his ______ presence is weak, and his speech is despised."”? (10:10, WEB) Medium
A) bodily B) wage C) presence D) don't
Answer: A. bodily. The passage specifies bodily: “For, "His letters," they say, "are weighty and strong, but his bodily presence is weak, and his speech is despised."”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “throwing down imaginations and every high thing that is exalted against the ______ of God, and bringing every thought into captivity to the obedience of Christ;” (10:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: knowledge. The cited words are “throwing down imaginations and every high thing that is exalted against the knowledge of God, and bringing every thought into captivity to the obedience of Christ;”
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… we are absent, such are we also in deed when we are present.” (10:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… we are absent, such are we also in deed when we are present.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For though I should ______ somewhat abundantly concerning our authority, (which the Lord gave for ______ you up, and not for casting you down) I will …” (10:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: boast; building. The wording is “For though I should boast somewhat abundantly concerning our authority, (which the Lord gave for building you up, and not for casting you down) I will …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “so as to preach the ______ even to the parts beyond you, not to boast in what someone else has already done.” (10:16); “… isn't he who commends himself who is approved, but whom the Lord ______.” (10:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: Gospel; commends. The first passage reads “so as to preach the Gospel even to the parts beyond you, not to boast in what someone else has already done.”; the second reads “… isn't he who commends himself who is approved, but whom the Lord commends.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… face? If anyone trusts in himself that he is Christ's, let him ______ this again with himself, that, even as he is Christ's, so also we …”? (10:7, WEB) Easy
A) readiness B) present C) faith D) consider
Answer: D. consider. 2 Corinthians 10:7 uses consider: “… face? If anyone trusts in himself that he is Christ's, let him consider this again with himself, that, even as he is Christ's, so also we …”
2 Corinthians 11
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For such men are false ______, ______ workers, masquerading as Christ's apostles.” (11:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: apostles; deceitful. The wording is “For such men are false apostles, deceitful workers, masquerading as Christ's apostles.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… though I am unskilled in speech, yet I am not unskilled in ______. No, in every way we have been revealed to you in all things.” (11:6, WEB) Easy
Answer: knowledge. The cited words are “… though I am unskilled in speech, yet I am not unskilled in knowledge. No, in every way we have been revealed to you in all things.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… wasn't a burden on anyone, for the brothers, when they came from ______, supplied the measure of my need. In everything I kept myself from being …”? (11:9, WEB) Easy
A) Ephesus B) Macedonia C) Persia D) Bethsaida
Answer: B. Macedonia. 2 Corinthians 11:9 uses Macedonia: “… wasn't a burden on anyone, for the brothers, when they came from Macedonia, supplied the measure of my need. In everything I kept myself from being …”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… bear with a man, if he brings you into bondage, if he rods you, if he takes you captive, if he exalts himself, if he strikes …” (11:20, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… bear with a man, if he brings you into bondage, if he devours you, if he takes you captive, if he exalts himself, if he strikes …” It says devours, not rods.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “It is no great thing therefore if his ministers also masquerade as ______ of righteousness, whose end will be according to their works.”? (11:15, WEB) Medium
A) mothers B) servants C) priests D) shepherd
Answer: B. servants. The passage specifies servants: “It is no great thing therefore if his ministers also masquerade as servants of righteousness, whose end will be according to their works.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… not weak? Who is caused to stumble, and I don't burn with ______?” (11:29); “In Damascus the governor under Aretas the ______ guarded the city of the Damascenes desiring to arrest me.” (11:32, WEB) Hard
Answer: indignation; king. The first passage reads “… not weak? Who is caused to stumble, and I don't burn with indignation?”; the second reads “In Damascus the governor under Aretas the king guarded the city of the Damascenes desiring to arrest me.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Are they servants of ______? (I speak as one beside himself) I am more so; in labors more abundantly, in prisons more abundantly, in stripes above …” (11:23); “… rivers, perils of robbers, perils from my countrymen, perils from the Gentiles, ______ in the city, perils in the wilderness, perils in the sea, perils among …” (11:26, WEB) Hard
A) Christ; Damascus B) Cornelius; perils C) perils; Christ D) Christ; perils
Answer: D. Christ; perils. Verse 23 reads “Are they servants of Christ? (I speak as one beside himself) I am more so; in labors more abundantly, in prisons more abundantly, in stripes above …”; verse 26 reads “… rivers, perils of robbers, perils from my countrymen, perils from the Gentiles, perils in the city, perils in the wilderness, perils in the sea, perils among …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “But I am afraid that somehow, as the serpent deceived ______ in his craftiness, so your minds might be corrupted from the simplicity that is in …” (11:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Eve. The verse reads, “But I am afraid that somehow, as the serpent deceived Eve in his craftiness, so your minds might be corrupted from the simplicity that is in …”
2 Corinthians 12
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… to boast, I will not be foolish; for I will speak the ______. But I forbear, so that no man may account of me above that …”? (12:6, WEB) Easy
A) knowledge B) glory C) peace D) truth
Answer: D. truth. 2 Corinthians 12:6 uses truth: “… to boast, I will not be foolish; for I will speak the truth. But I forbear, so that no man may account of me above that …”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “I know a man in Christ, ______ years ago (whether in the body, I don't know, or whether out of the body, I don't know; God …” (12:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: fourteen. The verse reads, “I know a man in Christ, fourteen years ago (whether in the body, I don't know, or whether out of the body, I don't know; God …”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Truly the signs of an apostle were worked among you in all patience, in signs and wonders and mighty works.” (12:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Truly the signs of an apostle were worked among you in all patience, in signs and wonders and mighty works.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… to me, "My ______ is sufficient for you, for my power is made perfect in weakness." Most gladly therefore I will rather ______ in my weaknesses, …” (12:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: grace; glory. The wording is “… to me, "My grace is sufficient for you, for my power is made perfect in weakness." Most gladly therefore I will rather glory in my weaknesses, …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: How does the answer to Paul's repeated plea reinterpret weakness? (12:7-10) Hard
A) The passage identifies the thorn beyond any interpretive debate. B) God's sufficient grace and power can be displayed without first removing the weakness. C) Paul is told his prayer proves he has no faith. D) God's power can never be present in weakness.
Answer: B. God's sufficient grace and power can be displayed without first removing the weakness. The thorn's precise identity is not stated with certainty. The passage should not be used to promise that faithful prayer always removes every bodily or circumstantial limitation.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… will most gladly spend and be spent for your souls. If I ______ you more abundantly, am I loved the less?” (12:15); “I exhorted ______, and I sent the brother with him. Did Titus take any advantage of you? Didn't we walk in the same spirit? Didn't we …” (12:18, WEB) Hard
A) love; Titus B) Titus; love C) love; Phoebe D) knowledge; Titus
Answer: A. love; Titus. Verse 15 reads “… will most gladly spend and be spent for your souls. If I love you more abundantly, am I loved the less?”; verse 18 reads “I exhorted Titus, and I sent the brother with him. Did Titus take any advantage of you? Didn't we walk in the same spirit? Didn't we …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Therefore I take pleasure in weaknesses, in injuries, in necessities, in ______, in distresses, for Christ's sake. For when I am weak, then am I strong.”? (12:10, WEB) Medium
A) whisperings B) persecutions C) hears D) unless
Answer: B. persecutions. The passage specifies persecutions: “Therefore I take pleasure in weaknesses, in injuries, in necessities, in persecutions, in distresses, for Christ's sake. For when I am weak, then am I strong.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… caught up into Paradise, and heard unspeakable words, which it is not ______ for a man to utter.” (12:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: lawful. The cited words are “… caught up into Paradise, and heard unspeakable words, which it is not lawful for a man to utter.”
2 Corinthians 13
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… pray to God that you do no evil; not that we may ______ approved, but that you may do that which is honorable, though we are …”? (13:7, WEB) Medium
A) truth B) established C) appear D) perfected
Answer: C. appear. The passage specifies appear: “… pray to God that you do no evil; not that we may appear approved, but that you may do that which is honorable, though we are …”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Test your own selves, whether you are in the ______. Test your own selves. Or don't you know as to your own selves, that Jesus Christ …”? (13:5, WEB) Easy
A) established B) write C) faith D) weakness
Answer: C. faith. 2 Corinthians 13:5 uses faith: “Test your own selves, whether you are in the faith. Test your own selves. Or don't you know as to your own selves, that Jesus Christ …”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… write to those who have sinned before now, and to all the ______, that, if I come again, I will not spare;” (13:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: rest. The verse reads, “… write to those who have sinned before now, and to all the rest, that, if I come again, I will not spare;”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “seeing that you seek a proof of ______ who speaks in me; who toward you is not weak, but is powerful in you.” (13:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Christ. The cited words are “seeing that you seek a proof of Christ who speaks in me; who toward you is not weak, but is powerful in you.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “But I ______ that you will know that we ______ disqualified.” (13:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: hope; aren't. The wording is “But I hope that you will know that we aren't disqualified.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… be of the same mind, live in peace, and the God of ______ and peace will be with you.” (13:11); “The ______ of the Lord Jesus Christ, the love of God, and the communion of the Holy Spirit, be with you all. Amen.” (13:14, WEB) Hard
A) wisdom; grace B) grace; love C) love; grace D) love; Jesus
Answer: C. love; grace. Verse 11 reads “… be of the same mind, live in peace, and the God of love and peace will be with you.”; verse 14 reads “The grace of the Lord Jesus Christ, the love of God, and the communion of the Holy Spirit, be with you all. Amen.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “For this cause I write these things while communion, that I may not deal sharply when present, according to the authority which the Lord gave me …” (13:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “For this cause I write these things while absent, that I may not deal sharply when present, according to the authority which the Lord gave me …” It says absent, not communion.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “All the saints ______ you.” (13:13); “Greet one another with a ______ kiss.” (13:12, WEB) Hard
Answer: greet; holy. The first passage reads “All the saints greet you.”; the second reads “Greet one another with a holy kiss.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Galatians
Galatians 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and there isn't another ______. Only there are some who trouble you, and want to pervert the Gospel of Christ.”? (1:7, WEB) Easy
A) countrymen B) gospel C) grace D) forever
Answer: B. gospel. Galatians 1:7 uses gospel: “and there isn't another gospel. Only there are some who trouble you, and want to pervert the Gospel of Christ.”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… my own age among my countrymen, being more exceedingly zealous for the days of my fathers.” (1:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… my own age among my countrymen, being more exceedingly zealous for the traditions of my fathers.” It says traditions, not days.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… For if I were still pleasing men, I wouldn't be a ______ of ______.” (1:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: servant; Christ. The wording is “… For if I were still pleasing men, I wouldn't be a servant of Christ.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______, an apostle (not from men, neither through man, but through Jesus Christ, and God the Father, who raised him from the dead),” (1:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Paul. The verse reads, “Paul, an apostle (not from men, neither through man, but through Jesus Christ, and God the Father, who raised him from the dead),”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… way of living in time past in the Jews' religion, how that ______ measure I persecuted the assembly of God, and ravaged it.”? (1:13, WEB) Medium
A) beyond B) confer C) pleasure D) except
Answer: A. beyond. The passage specifies beyond: “… way of living in time past in the Jews' religion, how that beyond measure I persecuted the assembly of God, and ravaged it.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “I was still unknown by ______ to the assemblies of Judea which were in Christ,” (1:22); “but they only heard: "He who once persecuted us now preaches the ______ that he once tried to destroy."” (1:23, WEB) Hard
Answer: face; faith. The first passage reads “I was still unknown by face to the assemblies of Judea which were in Christ,”; the second reads “but they only heard: "He who once persecuted us now preaches the faith that he once tried to destroy."”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “who gave himself for our ______, that he might deliver us out of this present evil age, according to the will of our God and Father--” (1:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: sins. The cited words are “who gave himself for our sins, that he might deliver us out of this present evil age, according to the will of our God and Father--”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… preach him among the Gentiles, I didn't immediately confer with flesh and ______,” (1:16); “Then after three years I went up to ______ to visit Peter, and stayed with him fifteen days.” (1:18, WEB) Hard
A) blood; Cana B) blood; Jerusalem C) eyes; Jerusalem D) Jerusalem; blood
Answer: B. blood; Jerusalem. Verse 16 reads “… preach him among the Gentiles, I didn't immediately confer with flesh and blood,”; verse 18 reads “Then after three years I went up to Jerusalem to visit Peter, and stayed with him fifteen days.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Galatians 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… who stole in to spy out our liberty which we have in ______ Jesus, that they might bring us into bondage;” (2:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: Christ. The cited words are “… who stole in to spy out our liberty which we have in Christ Jesus, that they might bring us into bondage;”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… preach among the Gentiles, but privately before those who were respected, for ______ that I might be running, or had run, in vain.” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: fear. The verse reads, “… preach among the Gentiles, but privately before those who were respected, for fear that I might be running, or had run, in vain.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “But from those who were reputed to be important (______ they were, it makes no difference to me; God doesn't show partiality to man)--they, I say, …”? (2:6, WEB) Easy
A) whatever B) Titus C) perceived D) bondage
Answer: A. whatever. Galatians 2:6 uses whatever: “But from those who were reputed to be important (whatever they were, it makes no difference to me; God doesn't show partiality to man)--they, I say, …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… was given to me, ______ and Cephas and John, they who were reputed to be pillars, gave to me and ______ the right hand of fellowship, …” (2:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: James; Barnabas. The wording is “… was given to me, James and Cephas and John, they who were reputed to be pillars, gave to me and Barnabas the right hand of fellowship, …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: How does Paul connect justification with the way Jewish and Gentile believers share fellowship? (2:11-21) Hard
A) Peter's withdrawal is praised as the gospel's ideal. B) Justification has no relation to treatment of other believers. C) The truth of grace in Christ challenges exclusion that pressures Gentiles to adopt Jewish identity markers. D) Paul says Gentiles must first become ethnically Jewish.
Answer: C. The truth of grace in Christ challenges exclusion that pressures Gentiles to adopt Jewish identity markers. The argument arises from a real table-fellowship dispute. It concerns both acceptance before God and the communal conduct that follows from that truth.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “But when ______ came to Antioch, I resisted him to the face, because he stood condemned.”? (2:11, WEB) Medium
A) Esther B) Nebuchadnezzar C) Andrew D) Peter
Answer: D. Peter. The passage specifies Peter: “But when Peter came to Antioch, I resisted him to the face, because he stood condemned.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… That life which I now live in the flesh, I live by ______ in the Son of God, who loved me, and gave himself up for …” (2:20); “I don't make void the ______ of God. For if righteousness is through the law, then Christ died for nothing!"” (2:21, WEB) Hard
Answer: faith; grace. The first passage reads “… That life which I now live in the flesh, I live by faith in the Son of God, who loved me, and gave himself up for …”; the second reads “I don't make void the grace of God. For if righteousness is through the law, then Christ died for nothing!"”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… ate with the Gentiles. But when they came, he drew back and carried himself, fearing those who were of the circumcision.” (2:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… ate with the Gentiles. But when they came, he drew back and separated himself, fearing those who were of the circumcision.” It says separated, not carried.
Galatians 3
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “that the blessing of ______ might come on the Gentiles through Christ Jesus; that we might receive the promise of the Spirit through faith.”? (3:14, WEB) Medium
A) Abraham B) Timothy C) Nahum D) Boaz
Answer: A. Abraham. The passage specifies Abraham: “that the blessing of Abraham might come on the Gentiles through Christ Jesus; that we might receive the promise of the Spirit through faith.”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Now I say this. A teaching confirmed beforehand by God in Christ, the law, which came four hundred thirty years after, does not annul, so as …” (3:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Now I say this. A covenant confirmed beforehand by God in Christ, the law, which came four hundred thirty years after, does not annul, so as …” It says covenant, not teaching.
Q3. Multiple choice: Why does Paul appeal to Abraham before discussing the law's later role? (3:6-18) Hard
A) Paul says the law retroactively cancels every promise. B) Abraham is said to belong to no part of the argument. C) The promise and the blessing of faith precede the Mosaic law. D) The promise was first made only after Sinai.
Answer: C. The promise and the blessing of faith precede the Mosaic law. Paul uses the biblical sequence to argue that the later law does not cancel God's promise. His Christian reading should still be understood as an argument about Israel's Scriptures and Gentile inclusion.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “I just want to learn this from you. Did you receive the ______ by the works of the law, or by hearing of faith?” (3:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Spirit. The verse reads, “I just want to learn this from you. Did you receive the Spirit by the works of the law, or by hearing of faith?”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “For you are all children of God, through faith in ______ Jesus.” (3:26); “… is neither male nor female; for you are all one in Christ ______.” (3:28, WEB) Hard
Answer: Christ; Jesus. The first passage reads “For you are all children of God, through faith in Christ Jesus.”; the second reads “… is neither male nor female; for you are all one in Christ Jesus.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… do it by the works of the law, or by hearing of ______?” (3:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: faith. The cited words are “… do it by the works of the law, or by hearing of faith?”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… faith, preached the Gospel beforehand to Abraham, saying, "In you all the ______ will be blessed."”? (3:8, WEB) Easy
A) hangs B) makes C) transgressions D) nations
Answer: D. nations. Galatians 3:8 uses nations: “… faith, preached the Gospel beforehand to Abraham, saying, "In you all the nations will be blessed."”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Now that no man is justified by the ______ before God is evident, for, "The ______ will live by faith."” (3:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: law; righteous. The wording is “Now that no man is justified by the law before God is evident, for, "The righteous will live by faith."” The missing terms belong in that order.
Galatians 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… we also, when we were children, were held in bondage under the ______ of the world.” (4:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: elements. The verse reads, “… we also, when we were children, were held in bondage under the elements of the world.”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “But it is always good to be zealous in a good cause, and not only when I am present with you.” (4:18, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “But it is always good to be zealous in a good cause, and not only when I am present with you.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “For it is written that ______ had two sons, one by the handmaid, and one by the free woman.” (4:22); “These things contain an allegory, for these are ______ covenants. One is from Mount Sinai, bearing children to bondage, which is Hagar.” (4:24, WEB) Hard
A) two; Abraham B) Abraham; two C) Abraham; ninety D) Silas; two
Answer: B. Abraham; two. Verse 22 reads “For it is written that Abraham had two sons, one by the handmaid, and one by the free woman.”; verse 24 reads “These things contain an allegory, for these are two covenants. One is from Mount Sinai, bearing children to bondage, which is Hagar.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… that because of weakness of the flesh I preached the ______ to you the ______ time.” (4:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: Gospel; first. The wording is “… that because of weakness of the flesh I preached the Gospel to you the first time.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… reject; but you received me as an angel of God, even as ______ Jesus.”? (4:14, WEB) Medium
A) Joshua B) Christ C) Andrew D) Obed
Answer: B. Christ. The passage specifies Christ: “… reject; but you received me as an angel of God, even as Christ Jesus.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… it is written, "Rejoice, you barren who don't bear. Break forth and ______, you that don't travail. For more are the children of the desolate than …” (4:27); “However what does the Scripture say? "______ out the handmaid and her son, for the son of the handmaid will not inherit with the son of …” (4:30, WEB) Hard
Answer: shout; Throw. The first passage reads “… it is written, "Rejoice, you barren who don't bear. Break forth and shout, you that don't travail. For more are the children of the desolate than …”; the second reads “However what does the Scripture say? "Throw out the handmaid and her son, for the son of the handmaid will not inherit with the son of …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “And because you are children, God sent out the ______ of his Son into your hearts, crying, "Abba, Father!"” (4:6, WEB) Easy
Answer: Spirit. The cited words are “And because you are children, God sent out the Spirit of his Son into your hearts, crying, "Abba, Father!"”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… now that you have come to know God, or rather to be ______ by God, why do you turn back again to the weak and miserable …”? (4:9, WEB) Easy
A) don't B) known C) temptation D) stewards

Galatians 5
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… think no other way. But he who troubles you will bear his ______, ______ he is.” (5:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: judgment; whoever. The wording is “… think no other way. But he who troubles you will bear his judgment, whoever he is.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: How does freedom in Christ relate to the fruit of the Spirit? (5:13-26) Hard
A) Freedom is defined as permission to harm a neighbor. B) The physical body is declared evil merely for existing. C) The Spirit's work is said to make love unnecessary. D) Freedom is expressed in serving through love and walking by the Spirit rather than indulging destructive desires.
Answer: D. Freedom is expressed in serving through love and walking by the Spirit rather than indulging destructive desires. The course links spiritual transformation with daily choices and love. Paul's contrast does not mean that every physical desire is evil simply because it is bodily.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For we, through the Spirit, by faith wait for the ______ of righteousness.” (5:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: hope. The cited words are “For we, through the Spirit, by faith wait for the hope of righteousness.”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… law is fulfilled in one word, in this: "You shall love your neighbor as yourself."” (5:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… law is fulfilled in one word, in this: "You shall love your neighbor as yourself."”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… Jesus neither circumcision amounts to anything, nor uncircumcision, but faith working through ______.”? (5:6, WEB) Easy
A) understanding B) faithfulness C) grief D) love
Answer: D. love. Galatians 5:6 uses love: “… Jesus neither circumcision amounts to anything, nor uncircumcision, but faith working through love.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… the fruit of the Spirit is love, joy, peace, patience, kindness, goodness, ______,” (5:22); “If we ______ by the Spirit, let's also walk by the Spirit.” (5:25, WEB) Hard
Answer: faithfulness; live. The first passage reads “… the fruit of the Spirit is love, joy, peace, patience, kindness, goodness, faithfulness,”; the second reads “If we live by the Spirit, let's also walk by the Spirit.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… use your freedom for gain to the flesh, but through love be ______ to one another.”? (5:13, WEB) Medium
A) shepherd B) servants C) brothers D) priests
Answer: B. servants. The passage specifies servants: “… use your freedom for gain to the flesh, but through love be servants to one another.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… who receives circumcision, that he is a debtor to do the whole ______.” (5:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: law. The verse reads, “… who receives circumcision, that he is a debtor to do the whole law.”

Answer: True. The actual wording is “… men, and especially toward those who are of the household of the faith.”

Answer: spirit. The verse reads, “… fault, you who are spiritual must restore such a one in a spirit of gentleness; looking to yourself so that you also aren't tempted.”

A) walk B) large C) due D) compel
Answer: C. due. The passage specifies due: “… us not be weary in doing good, for we will reap in due season, if we don't give up.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “For even they who receive circumcision don't keep the ______ themselves, but they desire to have you circumcised, that they may boast in your flesh.” (6:13); “But far be it from me to boast, except in the ______ of our Lord Jesus Christ, through which the world has been crucified to me, …” (6:14, WEB) Hard
A) law; cross B) cross; law C) law; God's D) instruction; cross
Answer: A. law; cross. Verse 13 reads “For even they who receive circumcision don't keep the law themselves, but they desire to have you circumcised, that they may boast in your flesh.”; verse 14 reads “But far be it from me to boast, except in the cross of our Lord Jesus Christ, through which the world has been crucified to me, …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… will from the flesh reap ______. But he who sows to the Spirit will from the Spirit reap eternal ______.” (6:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: corruption; life. The wording is “… will from the flesh reap corruption. But he who sows to the Spirit will from the Spirit reap eternal life.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Don't be deceived. God is not ______, for whatever a man sows, that will he also reap.”? (6:7, WEB) Easy
A) tempted B) mocked C) circumcised D) branded
Answer: B. mocked. Galatians 6:7 uses mocked: “Don't be deceived. God is not mocked, for whatever a man sows, that will he also reap.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… for I bear the marks of the Lord Jesus branded on my ______.” (6:17); “The ______ of our Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit, brothers. Amen.” (6:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: body; grace. The first passage reads “… for I bear the marks of the Lord Jesus branded on my body.”; the second reads “The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit, brothers. Amen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “But let each man test his own ______, and then he will take pride in himself and not in his neighbor.” (6:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: work. The cited words are “But let each man test his own work, and then he will take pride in himself and not in his neighbor.”
Ephesians
Ephesians 1
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… of our inheritance, to the redemption of God's own possession, to the worship of his glory.” (1:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… of our inheritance, to the redemption of God's own possession, to the praise of his glory.” It says praise, not worship.
Q2. Multiple choice: What should be distinguished when interpreting election and predestination in this blessing? (1:3-14) Hard
A) Adoption and redemption are absent from the blessing. B) The passage locates its blessings wholly apart from Christ. C) The passage celebrates God's purpose in Christ, while Christians differ over some accounts of how that purpose relates to human response. D) Every theological tradition draws identical conclusions.
Answer: C. The passage celebrates God's purpose in Christ, while Christians differ over some accounts of how that purpose relates to human response. The course discusses both sovereignty and responsibility. A quiz can test the stated blessings without grading every later Calvinist or Arminian inference as if the chapter explicitly names one system.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… blood, the forgiveness of our trespasses, according to the riches of his ______,”? (1:7, WEB) Easy
A) possession B) word C) heavens D) grace
Answer: D. grace. Ephesians 1:7 uses grace: “… blood, the forgiveness of our trespasses, according to the riches of his grace,”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… may give to you a spirit of wisdom and revelation in the ______ of him;” (1:17); “… the hope of his calling, and what are the riches of the ______ of his inheritance in the saints,” (1:18, WEB) Hard
A) joy; glory B) knowledge; grief C) knowledge; glory D) glory; knowledge
Answer: C. knowledge; glory. Verse 17 reads “… may give to you a spirit of wisdom and revelation in the knowledge of him;”; verse 18 reads “… the hope of his calling, and what are the riches of the glory of his inheritance in the saints,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______, an apostle of Christ Jesus through the will of God, to the saints who are at Ephesus, and the faithful in Christ Jesus:” (1:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Paul. The verse reads, “Paul, an apostle of Christ Jesus through the will of God, to the saints who are at Ephesus, and the faithful in Christ Jesus:”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “to an administration of the ______ of the times, to sum up all things in ______, the things in the heavens, and the things on the …” (1:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: fullness; Christ. The wording is “to an administration of the fullness of the times, to sum up all things in Christ, the things in the heavens, and the things on the …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “having predestined us for adoption as children through ______ Christ to himself, according to the good pleasure of his desire,” (1:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jesus. The cited words are “having predestined us for adoption as children through Jesus Christ to himself, according to the good pleasure of his desire,”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… of your salvation,--in whom, having also believed, you were sealed with the ______ Spirit of promise,”? (1:13, WEB) Medium
A) innocent B) guilty C) rich D) Holy
Answer: D. Holy. The passage specifies Holy: “… of your salvation,--in whom, having also believed, you were sealed with the Holy Spirit of promise,”
Ephesians 2
Q1. Multiple choice: How do grace, faith and good works fit together? (2:8-10) Hard
A) Salvation is God's gift, and those created anew in Christ are called to the good works God prepares. B) Paul says believers were created anew for no purpose. C) Grace means conduct can never matter. D) Good works are the price that forces God to save.
Answer: A. Salvation is God's gift, and those created anew in Christ are called to the good works God prepares. Works do not become a reason for boasting about earning salvation. The course pairs receiving grace with transformation rather than setting grace against every obedient response.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… the mind, and were by nature children of wrath, even as the ______.” (2:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: rest. The cited words are “… the mind, and were by nature children of wrath, even as the rest.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “and might reconcile them both in one body to God through the ______, having killed the hostility thereby.” (2:16); “So then you are no longer ______ and foreigners, but you are fellow citizens with the saints, and of the household of God,” (2:19, WEB) Hard
A) strangers; cross B) cross; strangers C) transgressions; strangers D) cross; saints
Answer: B. cross; strangers. Verse 16 reads “and might reconcile them both in one body to God through the cross, having killed the hostility thereby.”; verse 19 reads “So then you are no longer strangers and foreigners, but you are fellow citizens with the saints, and of the household of God,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… from the commonwealth of Israel, and strangers from the covenants of the ______, having no hope and without God in the world.”? (2:12, WEB) Medium
A) judgments B) promises C) promise D) testimony
Answer: C. promise. The passage specifies promise: “… from the commonwealth of Israel, and strangers from the covenants of the promise, having no hope and without God in the world.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the ages to come he might show the exceeding riches of his ______ in kindness toward us in Christ Jesus;”? (2:7, WEB) Easy
A) walk B) grace C) works D) built
Answer: B. grace. Ephesians 2:7 uses grace: “… the ages to come he might show the exceeding riches of his grace in kindness toward us in Christ Jesus;”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… world, according to the prince of the powers of the air, the ______ who now works in the children of disobedience;” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: spirit. The verse reads, “… world, according to the prince of the powers of the air, the spirit who now works in the children of disobedience;”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For we are his workmanship, created in ______ ______ for good works, which God prepared before that we would walk in them.” (2:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: Christ; Jesus. The wording is “For we are his workmanship, created in Christ Jesus for good works, which God prepared before that we would walk in them.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… he might create in himself one new man of the two, making peace;” (2:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… he might create in himself one new man of the two, making peace;”

Q2. Amen.” (3:21, WEB) Hard
Answer: knowledge; forever. The first passage reads “and to know Christ's love which surpasses knowledge, that you may be filled with all the fullness of God.”; the second reads “… the glory in the assembly and in Christ Jesus to all generations forever and ever. Amen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Answer: hidden; Christ. The wording is “… of the mystery which for ages has been hidden in God, who created all things through Jesus Christ;” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “that he would grant you, according to the riches of his ______, that you may be strengthened with power through his Spirit in the inward man;” (3:16); “… through faith; to the end that you, being rooted and grounded in ______,” (3:17, WEB) Hard
A) love; glory B) fear; love C) glory; glory D) glory; love
Answer: D. glory; love. Verse 16 reads “that he would grant you, according to the riches of his glory, that you may be strengthened with power through his Spirit in the inward man;”; verse 17 reads “… through faith; to the end that you, being rooted and grounded in love,”. Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… the children of men, as it has now been revealed to his ______ apostles and prophets in the Spirit;” (3:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: holy. The cited words are “… the children of men, as it has now been revealed to his holy apostles and prophets in the Spirit;”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Therefore I ask that you may not lose flesh at my troubles for you, which are your glory.” (3:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Therefore I ask that you may not lose heart at my troubles for you, which are your glory.” It says heart, not flesh.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For this cause I, ______, am the prisoner of Christ Jesus on behalf of you Gentiles,” (3:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Paul. The verse reads, “For this cause I, Paul, am the prisoner of Christ Jesus on behalf of you Gentiles,”

Ephesians 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… eager to keep the unity of the Spirit in the bond of ______.” (4:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: peace. The verse reads, “… eager to keep the unity of the Spirit in the bond of peace.”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “being darkened in their kindness, alienated from the life of God, because of the ignorance that is in them, because of the hardening of their hearts;” (4:18, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “being darkened in their understanding, alienated from the life of God, because of the ignorance that is in them, because of the hardening of their hearts;” It says understanding, not kindness.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Now this, "He ascended," what is it but that he also ______ descended into the lower parts of the earth?”? (4:9, WEB) Easy
A) three B) fifth C) sixty D) first
Answer: D. first. Ephesians 4:9 uses first: “Now this, "He ascended," what is it but that he also first descended into the lower parts of the earth?”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “until we all attain to the ______ of the faith, and of the ______ of the Son of God, to a full grown man, to the …” (4:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: unity; knowledge. The wording is “until we all attain to the unity of the faith, and of the knowledge of the Son of God, to a full grown man, to the …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: How does the body image connect different gifts with maturity? (4:1-16) Hard
A) Maturity means remaining vulnerable to every teaching. B) Different ministries equip the saints and build the body toward unity and maturity in Christ. C) Only one visible leader is allowed to do meaningful service. D) Difference of gifts makes unity impossible.
Answer: B. Different ministries equip the saints and build the body toward unity and maturity in Christ. The course understands church as a people whose members serve one another. Unity is not defined by making every member's work identical.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… according to the working in measure of each individual part, makes the ______ increase to the building up of itself in love.”? (4:16, WEB) Medium
A) grace B) Christ C) body D) fitted
Answer: C. body. The passage specifies body: “… according to the working in measure of each individual part, makes the body increase to the building up of itself in love.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Let him who stole ______ no more; but rather let him labor, working with his hands the thing that is good, that he may have something …” (4:28); “Let all bitterness, wrath, anger, ______, and slander, be put away from you, with all malice.” (4:31, WEB) Hard
Answer: steal; outcry. The first passage reads “Let him who stole steal no more; but rather let him labor, working with his hands the thing that is good, that he may have something …”; the second reads “Let all bitterness, wrath, anger, outcry, and slander, be put away from you, with all malice.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Therefore he says, "When he ______ on high, he led captivity captive, and gave gifts to men."” (4:8, WEB) Easy
Answer: ascended. The cited words are “Therefore he says, "When he ascended on high, he led captivity captive, and gave gifts to men."”
Ephesians 5
Q1. Multiple choice: What context must accompany the household instructions? (5:18-33) Hard
A) Life filled by the Spirit, mutual submission and the husband's obligation to Christlike self-giving love. B) The household discussion is unrelated to life in the Spirit. C) Husbands are exempt from Christlike love. D) A spouse's power makes abusive conduct righteous.
Answer: A. Life filled by the Spirit, mutual submission and the husband's obligation to Christlike self-giving love. Christians debate the precise ordering of roles. The passage never gives permission for abuse, coercion or neglect of the command to love as Christ gives Himself.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the fruit of the Spirit is in all goodness and righteousness and ______,”? (5:9, WEB) Easy
A) truth B) shame C) honor D) wrath
Answer: A. truth. Ephesians 5:9 uses truth: “… the fruit of the Spirit is in all goodness and righteousness and truth,”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… loved you, and gave himself up for us, an offering and a ______ to God for a sweet-smelling fragrance.” (5:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: sacrifice. The verse reads, “… loved you, and gave himself up for us, an offering and a sacrifice to God for a sweet-smelling fragrance.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… nor unclean person, nor covetous man, who is an idolater, has any ______ in the Kingdom of Christ and God.” (5:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: inheritance. The cited words are “… nor unclean person, nor covetous man, who is an idolater, has any inheritance in the Kingdom of Christ and God.”
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… in psalms, hymns, and spiritual songs; singing, and singing praises in your heart to the Lord;” (5:19, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… in psalms, hymns, and spiritual songs; singing, and singing praises in your heart to the Lord;”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “But all things, when they are ______, are revealed by the light, for everything that is revealed is ______.” (5:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: reproved; light. The wording is “But all things, when they are reproved, are revealed by the light, for everything that is revealed is light.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “For no man ever hated his own flesh; but nourishes and ______ it, even as the Lord also does the assembly;” (5:29); “… his father and mother, and will be joined to his wife. The ______ will become one flesh."” (5:31, WEB) Hard
Answer: cherishes; two. The first passage reads “For no man ever hated his own flesh; but nourishes and cherishes it, even as the Lord also does the assembly;”; the second reads “… his father and mother, and will be joined to his wife. The two will become one flesh."”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Therefore ______ carefully how you walk, not as unwise, but as wise;”? (5:15, WEB) Medium
A) word B) pleasing C) watch D) cleansed
Answer: C. watch. The passage specifies watch: “Therefore watch carefully how you walk, not as unwise, but as wise;”
Ephesians 6
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… against the principalities, against the powers, against the world's rulers of the ______ of this age, and against the spiritual hosts of wickedness in the heavenly …”? (6:12, WEB) Medium

Answer: C. darkness. The passage specifies darkness: “… against the principalities, against the powers, against the world's rulers of the darkness of this age, and against the spiritual hosts of wickedness in the heavenly …”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “with all prayer and requests, praying at all times in the ______, and being watchful to this end in all perseverance and requests for all the …” (6:18); “… opening my mouth, to make known with boldness the mystery of the ______,” (6:19, WEB) Hard
A) whole; Gospel B) Spirit; Gospel C) Spirit; provoke D) Gospel; Spirit
Answer: B. Spirit; Gospel. Verse 18 reads “with all prayer and requests, praying at all times in the Spirit, and being watchful to this end in all perseverance and requests for all the …”; verse 19 reads “… opening my mouth, to make known with boldness the mystery of the Gospel,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… of service only when eyes are on you, as men-pleasers; but as ______ of Christ, doing the will of God from the heart;”? (6:6, WEB) Easy

Answer: True. The actual wording is “Stand therefore, having the utility belt of truth buckled around your waist, and having put on the breastplate of righteousness,”
Q7. Philippians 2
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… if I am poured out on the sacrifice and service of your life, I rejoice, and rejoice with you all.” (2:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… if I am poured out on the sacrifice and service of your faith, I rejoice, and rejoice with you all.” It says faith, not life.
Q2. Multiple choice: How does the account of Christ's humility serve the appeal to the congregation? (2:1-11) Hard
A) Christ's example has no bearing on relationships. B) His self-giving becomes the pattern for attention to others rather than rivalry and self-importance. C) Humility is described as the pursuit of personal status. D) Paul explicitly says Christ stopped being divine.
Answer: B. His self-giving becomes the pattern for attention to others rather than rivalry and self-importance. The course reads the passage with Christ's full humanity and divinity in view. Emptying Himself should not casually be paraphrased as ceasing to be God.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “that you may become blameless and ______, children of God without blemish in the midst of a crooked and perverse generation, among whom you are seen …”? (2:15, WEB) Medium

Answer: C. harmless. The passage specifies harmless: “that you may become blameless and harmless, children of God without blemish in the midst of a crooked and perverse generation, among whom you are seen …”

Q5. Philippians 4
Q1. Multiple choice: What circumstances frame Paul's claim that he can do all things through the One who strengthens him? (4:10-13) Hard
A) He promises victory in every competition. B) He says he has never experienced hunger or need. C) He describes contentment as depending entirely on wealth. D) He is discussing learned contentment in both need and abundance.
Answer: D. He is discussing learned contentment in both need and abundance. The statement is not an unlimited guarantee of success at every chosen project. Its context concerns dependence and endurance across changing material circumstances.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… true yoke-fellow, help these women, for they labored with me in the ______, with Clement also, and the rest of my fellow workers, whose names are …” (4:3, WEB) Easy

A) learned B) beloved C) need D) indeed
Answer: A. learned. The passage specifies learned: “… also how to abound. In everything and in all things I have learned the secret both to be filled and to be hungry, both to abound …”

Colossians
Colossians 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “even as you learned of Epaphras our beloved fellow ______, who is a faithful minister of Christ on our behalf,”? (1:7, WEB) Easy
A) king B) servant C) mothers D) brothers
Answer: B. servant. Colossians 1:7 uses servant: “even as you learned of Epaphras our beloved fellow servant, who is a faithful minister of Christ on our behalf,”
Q2. Multiple choice: How does the description of Christ as firstborn of creation relate to the following lines? (1:15-20) Hard
A) The passage gives every created power authority over Christ. B) He is presented as supreme over creation, with all things created through Him and for Him. C) The following lines say Christ is unrelated to creation. D) Firstborn must here mean that God created Jesus before making everything else.
Answer: B. He is presented as supreme over creation, with all things created through Him and for Him. The surrounding statements govern the title's meaning. In the course's Christology, the phrase does not identify Jesus as merely the first created being.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “to the saints and ______ brothers in Christ at Colossae: Grace to you and peace from God our Father, and the Lord Jesus Christ.” (1:2, WEB) Easy

Q4. Multiple choice: How does new identity in Christ affect the old self and communal conduct? (3:1-17) Hard
A) New identity leaves all former conduct untouched. B) Believers put away destructive practices and put on compassion, humility, forgiveness and love. C) Material success becomes the sole ground of worth. D) Forgiveness is excluded from the renewed community.
Answer: B. Believers put away destructive practices and put on compassion, humility, forgiveness and love. The course contrasts identity grounded in Christ with identity grounded only in possessions or approval. New identity is meant to shape concrete habits and relationships.

The first passage reads “Fathers, don't provoke your children, so that they won't be discouraged.”; the second reads “knowing that from the Lord you will receive the reward of the inheritance; for you serve the Lord Christ.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Epaphras, who is one of you, a mothers of Christ, salutes you, always striving for you in his prayers, that you may stand perfect and complete …” (4:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Epaphras, who is one of you, a servant of Christ, salutes you, always striving for you in his prayers, that you may stand perfect and complete …” It says servant, not mothers.
1 Thessalonians
1 Thessalonians 1
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “We know, brothers loved by God, that you are ______,” (1:4); “We ______ give thanks to God for all of you, mentioning you in our prayers,” (1:2, WEB) Hard
Answer: chosen; always. The first passage reads “We know, brothers loved by God, that you are chosen,”; the second reads “We always give thanks to God for all of you, mentioning you in our prayers,”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and that our ______ came to you not in word only, but also in power, and in the Holy Spirit, and with much assurance. You know …”? (1:5, WEB) Easy
A) believe B) mentioning C) wrath D) Gospel
Answer: D. Gospel. 1 Thessalonians 1:5 uses Gospel: “and that our Gospel came to you not in word only, but also in power, and in the Holy Spirit, and with much assurance. You know …”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… of the Lord, having received the word in much affliction, with ______ of the ______ Spirit,” (1:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: joy; Holy. The wording is “… of the Lord, having received the word in much affliction, with joy of the Holy Spirit,” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… sounded forth the word of the Lord, not only in Macedonia and ______, but also in every place your faith toward God has gone forth; so …”? (1:8, WEB) Medium
A) Achaia B) Damascus C) Israel D) Thessalonica
Answer: A. Achaia. The passage specifies Achaia: “… sounded forth the word of the Lord, not only in Macedonia and Achaia, but also in every place your faith toward God has gone forth; so …”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… had from you; and how you turned to God from idols, to ______ a living and true God,” (1:9); “… wait for his Son from heaven, whom he raised from the dead-- ______, who delivers us from the wrath to come.” (1:10, WEB) Hard
A) Jesus; serve B) serve; Jesus C) always; Jesus D) serve; Nehemiah
Answer: B. serve; Jesus. Verse 9 reads “… had from you; and how you turned to God from idols, to serve a living and true God,”; verse 10 reads “… wait for his Son from heaven, whom he raised from the dead-- Jesus, who delivers us from the wrath to come.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “remembering without ceasing your work of ______ and labor of love and patience of hope in our Lord Jesus Christ, before our God and Father.” (1:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: faith. The cited words are “remembering without ceasing your work of faith and labor of love and patience of hope in our Lord Jesus Christ, before our God and Father.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… of the Thessalonians in God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ: ______ to you and peace from God our Father and the Lord Jesus Christ.” (1:1, WEB) Easy

Answer: True. The actual wording is “so that you became an example to all who believe in Macedonia and in Achaia.”

Answer: A. holy. The passage specifies holy: “You are witnesses with God, how holy, righteously, and blamelessly we behaved ourselves toward you who believe.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “but having suffered before and been shamefully treated, as you know, at ______, we grew bold in our God to tell you the Gospel of God …” (2:2, WEB) Easy

Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Even so, affectionately ______ for you, we were well ______ to impart to you, not the Gospel of God only, but also our own souls, because …” (2:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: longing; pleased. The wording is “Even so, affectionately longing for you, we were well pleased to impart to you, not the Gospel of God only, but also our own souls, because …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… being bereaved of you for a short season, in presence, not in ______, tried even harder to see your face with great desire,” (2:17); “For what is our ______, or joy, or crown of rejoicing? Isn't it even you, before our Lord Jesus at his coming?” (2:19, WEB) Hard
Answer: heart; hope. Isn't it even you, before our Lord Jesus at his coming?”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Q6. 1 Thessalonians 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… who rejects doesn't reject man, but God, who has also given his ______ ______ to you.” (4:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: Holy; Spirit. The wording is “… who rejects doesn't reject man, but God, who has also given his Holy Spirit to you.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: What pastoral aim frames the teaching about believers who have died? (4:13-18) Hard
A) Its stated purpose is to calculate a date. B) The teaching forbids all grief. C) To give hope and mutual encouragement rather than grief without hope. D) Paul says dead believers have no hope in Christ.
Answer: C. To give hope and mutual encouragement rather than grief without hope. Christians differ about some details of the gathering and its relation to other end-time passages. Paul's explicit conclusion is that the believers should encourage one another.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the Lord is an avenger in all these things, as also we ______ you and testified.”? (4:6, WEB) Easy
A) indeed B) charged C) testified D) forewarned
Answer: D. forewarned. 1 Thessalonians 4:6 uses forewarned: “… the Lord is an avenger in all these things, as also we forewarned you and testified.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… know how to possess himself of his own vessel in sanctification and ______,” (4:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: honor. The cited words are “… know how to possess himself of his own vessel in sanctification and honor,”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “But concerning brotherly ______, you have no need that one write to you. For you yourselves are taught by God to love one another,”? (4:9, WEB) Medium
A) joy B) wrath C) love D) glory
Answer: C. love. The passage specifies love: “But concerning brotherly love, you have no need that one write to you. For you yourselves are taught by God to love one another,”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Finally then, brothers, we beg and exhort you in the Lord ______, that as you received from us how you ought to walk and to please …” (4:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jesus. The verse reads, “Finally then, brothers, we beg and exhort you in the Lord Jesus, that as you received from us how you ought to walk and to please …”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “and that you make it your ambition to lead a quiet life, and to do your own business, and to work with your own hands, even …” (4:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “and that you make it your ambition to lead a quiet life, and to do your own business, and to work with your own hands, even …”
Q8. A) rest; reject B) praise; fallen C) fallen; rest D) rest; fallen

Answer: True. The actual wording is “… everything give thanks, for this is the will of God in Christ Jesus toward you.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Don't quench the ______.” (5:19); “… of peace himself sanctify you completely. May your whole spirit, soul, and ______ be preserved blameless at the coming of our Lord Jesus Christ.” (5:23, WEB) Hard

2 Thessalonians 2
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… lawless one will be revealed, whom the Lord will kill with the ______ of his mouth, and bring to nothing by the brightness of his coming;”? (2:8, WEB) Medium
A) Spirit B) breath C) revealed D) shaken
Answer: B. breath. The passage specifies breath: “… lawless one will be revealed, whom the Lord will kill with the breath of his mouth, and bring to nothing by the brightness of his coming;”
Q2. Multiple choice: What immediate error does Paul oppose before describing the man of lawlessness? (2:1-3) Hard
A) The confession that Jesus will return. B) The claim that the community has received any letter at all. C) The claim that the day of the Lord has already arrived. D) The view that believers should stand firm.
Answer: C. The claim that the day of the Lord has already arrived. The passage aims to steady a disturbed community. Detailed identifications of the figures and chronology differ among Christians; a quiz should not silently make a modern identification the text's only possible meaning.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… loved by the Lord, because God chose you from the beginning for ______ through sanctification of the Spirit and belief in the truth;” (2:13); “to which he called you through our ______, for the obtaining of the glory of our Lord Jesus Christ.” (2:14, WEB) Hard
A) salvation; comes B) Gospel; salvation C) salvation; Gospel D) praise; Gospel
Answer: C. salvation; Gospel. Verse 13 reads “… loved by the Lord, because God chose you from the beginning for salvation through sanctification of the Spirit and belief in the truth;”; verse 14 reads “to which he called you through our Gospel, for the obtaining of the glory of our Lord Jesus Christ.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… wickedness for those who are being lost, because they didn't receive the righteousness of the truth, that they might be saved.” (2:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… wickedness for those who are being lost, because they didn't receive the love of the truth, that they might be saved.” It says love, not righteousness.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For the mystery of ______ already works. Only there is one who restrains now, until he is taken out of the ______.” (2:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: lawlessness; way. The wording is “For the mystery of lawlessness already works. Only there is one who restrains now, until he is taken out of the way.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… word, or by letter as from us, saying that the day of ______ had come.” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Christ. The verse reads, “… word, or by letter as from us, saying that the day of Christ had come.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… or that is worshiped; so that he sits as God in the ______ of God, setting himself up as God.”? (2:4, WEB) Easy
A) basin B) house C) temple D) throne
Answer: C. temple. 2 Thessalonians 2:4 uses temple: “… or that is worshiped; so that he sits as God in the temple of God, setting himself up as God.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… will not be, unless the departure comes first, and the man of ______ is revealed, the son of destruction,” (2:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: sin. The cited words are “… will not be, unless the departure comes first, and the man of sin is revealed, the son of destruction,”

1 Timothy 1
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… him who enabled me, Christ Jesus our Lord, because he counted me pure, appointing me to service;” (1:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… him who enabled me, Christ Jesus our Lord, because he counted me faithful, appointing me to service;” It says faithful, not pure.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The grace of our Lord abounded exceedingly with faith and ______ which is in Christ Jesus.” (1:14); “However, for this cause I obtained mercy, that in me ______, Jesus Christ might display all his patience, for an example of those who were going …” (1:16, WEB) Hard
A) first; love B) love; first C) love; four D) peace; first
Answer: B. love; first. Verse 14 reads “The grace of our Lord abounded exceedingly with faith and love which is in Christ Jesus.”; verse 16 reads “However, for this cause I obtained mercy, that in me first, Jesus Christ might display all his patience, for an example of those who were going …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… endless genealogies, which cause disputes, rather than God's stewardship, which is in ______--” (1:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: faith. The cited words are “… endless genealogies, which cause disputes, rather than God's stewardship, which is in faith--”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “to Timothy, my true child in faith: Grace, mercy, and ______, from God our Father and Christ Jesus our Lord.” (1:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: peace. The verse reads, “to Timothy, my true child in faith: Grace, mercy, and peace, from God our Father and Christ Jesus our Lord.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “This charge I commit to you, my child ______, according to the prophecies which led the way to you, that by them you may wage the …” (1:18); “of whom is Hymenaeus and Alexander; whom I ______ to Satan, that they might be taught not to blaspheme.” (1:20, WEB) Hard
Answer: Timothy; delivered.
