Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “as knowing this, that ______ is not made for a ______ man, but for the lawless and insubordinate, for the ungodly and sinners, for the unholy …” (1:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: law; righteous. The wording is “as knowing this, that law is not made for a righteous man, but for the lawless and insubordinate, for the ungodly and sinners, for the unholy …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the charge is love, out of a pure heart and a good ______ and unfeigned faith;”? (1:5, WEB) Easy
A) peace B) conscience C) murderers D) unbelief
Answer: B. conscience. 1 Timothy 1:5 uses conscience: “… the charge is love, out of a pure heart and a good conscience and unfeigned faith;”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… for liars, for perjurers, and for any other thing contrary to the ______ doctrine;”? (1:10, WEB) Medium
A) sexually B) display C) sound D) worthy
Answer: C. sound. The passage specifies sound: “… for liars, for perjurers, and for any other thing contrary to the sound doctrine;”
1 Timothy 2
Q1. Multiple choice: Why should the instructions about teaching and authority be explained with interpretive care? (2:8-15) Hard
A) The surrounding chapter has no concern for prayer or conduct. B) The text plainly teaches that bearing children purchases salvation. C) Christians differ over the restriction's scope, its setting and the meaning of several terms. D) Every tradition applies every detail identically.
Answer: C. Christians differ over the restriction's scope, its setting and the meaning of several terms. The question should distinguish what Paul says from applications about every ministry role. The difficult reference to salvation through childbearing should not be turned into a claim that women earn salvation by having children.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “I desire therefore that the men in every place pray, lifting up ______ hands without wrath and doubting.”? (2:8, WEB) Medium
A) proud B) wicked C) rich D) holy
Answer: D. holy. The passage specifies holy: “I desire therefore that the men in every place pray, lifting up holy hands without wrath and doubting.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… all people to be saved and come to full knowledge of the ______.” (2:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: truth. The cited words are “… all people to be saved and come to full knowledge of the truth.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… a preacher and an ______ (I am telling the truth in Christ, not lying), a teacher of the Gentiles in ______ and truth.” (2:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: apostle; faith. The wording is “… a preacher and an apostle (I am telling the truth in Christ, not lying), a teacher of the Gentiles in faith and truth.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “for ______ and all who are in high places; that we may lead a tranquil and quiet life in all godliness and reverence.” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: kings. The verse reads, “for kings and all who are in high places; that we may lead a tranquil and quiet life in all godliness and reverence.”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… in decent clothing, with modesty and propriety; not just with braided hair, clay, pearls, or expensive clothing;” (2:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… in decent clothing, with modesty and propriety; not just with braided hair, gold, pearls, or expensive clothing;” It says gold, not clay.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… is one God, and one mediator between God and men, the man ______ Jesus,”? (2:5, WEB) Easy
A) Stephen B) Christ C) Zophar D) Nebuchadnezzar
Answer: B. Christ. 1 Timothy 2:5 uses Christ: “… is one God, and one mediator between God and men, the man Christ Jesus,”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “But I don't permit a ______ to teach, nor to exercise authority over a man, but to be in quietness.” (2:12); “______ wasn't deceived, but the woman, being deceived, has fallen into disobedience;” (2:14, WEB) Hard
A) doubting; Adam B) Adam; woman C) woman; Adam D) woman; Luke
Answer: C. woman; Adam. Verse 12 reads “But I don't permit a woman to teach, nor to exercise authority over a man, but to be in quietness.”; verse 14 reads “Adam wasn't deceived, but the woman, being deceived, has fallen into disobedience;”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
1 Timothy 3

Answer: testimony; snare. The wording is “Moreover he must have good testimony from those who are outside, to avoid falling into reproach and the snare of the devil.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: What pattern dominates the stated qualifications for overseers and deacons? (3:1-13) Hard
A) Money is the central test for every leader. B) Personal fame automatically proves qualification. C) Conduct toward family and others is declared irrelevant. D) Demonstrated character, responsible conduct and faithfulness rather than wealth or celebrity.
Answer: D. Demonstrated character, responsible conduct and faithfulness rather than wealth or celebrity. The course stresses character and service in church leadership. Traditions differ about some role and gender applications, but impressive status is not the primary qualification listed.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… the assembly of the living God, the pillar and ground of the ______.” (3:15); “… the flesh, Justified in the spirit, Seen by angels, Preached among the ______, Believed on in the world, And received up in glory.” (3:16, WEB) Hard
Answer: truth; nations. The first passage reads “… the assembly of the living God, the pillar and ground of the truth.”; the second reads “… the flesh, Justified in the spirit, Seen by angels, Preached among the nations, Believed on in the world, And received up in glory.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Let them also second be tested; then let them serve as deacons, if they are blameless.” (3:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Let them also first be tested; then let them serve as deacons, if they are blameless.” It says first, not second.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The overseer therefore must be without reproach, the husband of one ______, temperate, sensible, modest, hospitable, good at teaching;” (3:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: wife. The verse reads, “The overseer therefore must be without reproach, the husband of one wife, temperate, sensible, modest, hospitable, good at teaching;”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… the same way, must be reverent, not double-tongued, not addicted to much ______, not greedy for money;”? (3:8, WEB) Medium
A) figs B) wine C) barley D) grain
Answer: B. wine. The passage specifies wine: “… the same way, must be reverent, not double-tongued, not addicted to much wine, not greedy for money;”

Answer: drinker. The cited words are “not a drinker, not violent, not greedy for money, but gentle, not quarrelsome, not covetous;”

Answer: conscience. The verse reads, “through the hypocrisy of men who speak lies, branded in their own conscience as with a hot iron;”

Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “For to this end we both labor and suffer reproach, because we have set our trust in the living God, who is the Savior of all …” (4:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “For to this end we both labor and suffer reproach, because we have set our trust in the living God, who is the Savior of all …”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… has some value, but godliness has value for all things, having the ______ of the life which is now, and of that which is to come.”? (4:8, WEB) Medium

1 Timothy 6
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “constant friction of people of corrupt minds and destitute of the ______, who suppose that godliness is a means of gain. Withdraw yourself from such.”? (6:5, WEB) Easy
A) fear B) truth C) wisdom D) grief
Answer: B. truth. 1 Timothy 6:5 uses truth: “constant friction of people of corrupt minds and destitute of the truth, who suppose that godliness is a means of gain. Withdraw yourself from such.”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… determined to be ______ fall into a temptation and a snare and many foolish and harmful lusts, such as drown men in ______ and destruction.” (6:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: rich; ruin. The wording is “… determined to be rich fall into a temptation and a snare and many foolish and harmful lusts, such as drown men in ruin and destruction.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “For the ______ of money is a root of all kinds of evil. Some have been led astray from the faith in their greed, and have …”? (6:10, WEB) Medium
A) anger B) understanding C) love D) joy
Answer: C. love. The passage specifies love: “For the love of money is a root of all kinds of evil. Some have been led astray from the faith in their greed, and have …”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Fight the good fight of fall. Lay hold of the eternal life to which you were called, and you confessed the good confession in the sight …” (6:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Fight the good fight of faith. Lay hold of the eternal life to which you were called, and you confessed the good confession in the sight …” It says faith, not fall.
Q5. Multiple choice: How does the warning about the love of money differ from a claim that money itself is the root of every evil? (6:6-10, 17-19) Hard
A) It warns against misplaced desire and reliance on wealth while commanding generosity. B) Money itself is identified as the cause of every possible sin. C) The rich are told to refuse all generosity. D) Wealth automatically proves spiritual maturity.
Answer: A. It warns against misplaced desire and reliance on wealth while commanding generosity. Paul addresses contentment, desire and the responsibilities of the rich. The text does not say every possession is intrinsically evil or that wealth proves God's favor.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “he is conceited, knowing nothing, but obsessed with arguments, ______, and word battles, from which come envy, strife, reviling, evil suspicions,” (6:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: disputes. The cited words are “he is conceited, knowing nothing, but obsessed with arguments, disputes, and word battles, from which come envy, strife, reviling, evil suspicions,”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Those who have believing masters, let them not ______ them, because they are brothers, but rather let them serve them, because those who partake of the …” (6:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: despise. The verse reads, “Those who have believing masters, let them not despise them, because they are brothers, but rather let them serve them, because those who partake of the …”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… times he will show, who is the blessed and only Ruler, the ______ of kings, and Lord of lords;” (6:15); “… in this present world that they not be haughty, nor have their ______ set on the uncertainty of riches, but on the living God, who richly …” (6:17, WEB) Hard
A) hope; King B) King; hope C) King; righteousness D) husband; hope
Answer: B. King; hope. Verse 15 reads “… times he will show, who is the blessed and only Ruler, the King of kings, and Lord of lords;”; verse 17 reads “… in this present world that they not be haughty, nor have their hope set on the uncertainty of riches, but on the living God, who richly …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Q2. 2 Timothy 3
Q1. Multiple choice: What purpose accompanies the claim that Scripture is God-breathed? (3:14-17) Hard
A) Scripture is useful only to make someone win arguments. B) Its purpose excludes transformed conduct. C) It equips God's servant through teaching, correction and training for good works. D) Correction and training are explicitly rejected.
Answer: C. It equips God's servant through teaching, correction and training for good works. Paul joins Scripture's authority with formation and service. The course's emphasis on practice fits this stated purpose; the text is not merely an invitation to accumulate information.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “persecutions, and sufferings: those things that happened to me at Capernaum, Iconium, and Lystra. I endured those persecutions. Out of them all the Lord delivered me.” (3:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “persecutions, and sufferings: those things that happened to me at Antioch, Iconium, and Lystra. I endured those persecutions. Out of them all the Lord delivered me.” It says Antioch, not Capernaum.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… opposed Moses, so do these also oppose the ______; men corrupted in mind, reprobate concerning the ______.” (3:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: truth; faith. The wording is “… opposed Moses, so do these also oppose the truth; men corrupted in mind, reprobate concerning the faith.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “without natural affection, unforgiving, slanderers, without self-control, ______, no lovers of good,” (3:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: fierce. The cited words are “without natural affection, unforgiving, slanderers, without self-control, fierce, no lovers of good,”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… who creep into houses, and take captive gullible women loaded down with ______, led away by various lusts,”? (3:6, WEB) Easy
A) sins B) iniquity C) wickedness D) transgression
Answer: A. sins. 2 Timothy 3:6 uses sins: “… who creep into houses, and take captive gullible women loaded down with sins, led away by various lusts,”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For men will be lovers of self, lovers of money, boastful, ______, blasphemers, disobedient to parents, unthankful, unholy,” (3:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: arrogant. The verse reads, “For men will be lovers of self, lovers of money, boastful, arrogant, blasphemers, disobedient to parents, unthankful, unholy,”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… for teaching, for reproof, for correction, and for instruction which is in ______,” (3:16); “that the man of God may be complete, thoroughly ______ for every good work.” (3:17, WEB) Hard
Answer: righteousness; equipped. The first passage reads “… for teaching, for reproof, for correction, and for instruction which is in righteousness,”; the second reads “that the man of God may be complete, thoroughly equipped for every good work.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “But you did follow my teaching, conduct, purpose, faith, patience, ______, steadfastness,”? (3:10, WEB) Medium
A) joy B) love C) mercy D) righteousness
Answer: B. love. The passage specifies love: “But you did follow my teaching, conduct, purpose, faith, patience, love, steadfastness,”
2 Timothy 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Erastus remained at ______, but I left Trophimus at Miletus sick.” (4:20); “The Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit. ______ be with you. Amen.” (4:22, WEB) Hard
Answer: Corinth; Grace. The first passage reads “Erastus remained at Corinth, but I left Trophimus at Miletus sick.”; the second reads “The Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit. Grace be with you. Amen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Only Luke is with me. Take ______, and bring him with you, for he is useful to me for ministering.”? (4:11, WEB) Medium
A) Jonathan B) Mark C) Syntyche D) Cornelius
Answer: B. Mark. The passage specifies Mark: “Only Luke is with me. Take Mark, and bring him with you, for he is useful to me for ministering.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “and will turn away their ears from the ______, and turn aside to fables.” (4:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: truth. The cited words are “and will turn away their ears from the truth, and turn aside to fables.”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Bring the cloak that I left at Laodicea with Carpus when you come, and the books, especially the parchments.” (4:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Bring the cloak that I left at Troas with Carpus when you come, and the books, especially the parchments.” It says Troas, not Laodicea.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… left me, having loved this present world, and went to ______; Crescens to Galatia, and ______ to Dalmatia.” (4:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: Thessalonica; Titus. The wording is “… left me, having loved this present world, and went to Thessalonica; Crescens to Galatia, and Titus to Dalmatia.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “From now on, there is stored up for me the crown of ______, which the Lord, the righteous judge, will give to me on that day; …”? (4:8, WEB) Easy
A) justice B) righteousness C) faithfulness D) mercy
Answer: B. righteousness. 2 Timothy 4:8 uses righteousness: “From now on, there is stored up for me the crown of righteousness, which the Lord, the righteous judge, will give to me on that day; …”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “preach the word; be urgent in ______ and out of season; reprove, rebuke, and exhort, with all patience and teaching.” (4:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: season. The verse reads, “preach the word; be urgent in season and out of season; reprove, rebuke, and exhort, with all patience and teaching.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… all the Gentiles might hear; and I was delivered out of the ______ of the lion.” (4:17); “And the Lord will ______ me from every evil work, and will preserve me for his heavenly Kingdom; to whom be the glory forever and ever. …” (4:18, WEB) Hard
A) mouth; aside B) deliver; mouth C) mouth; deliver D) lips; deliver
Answer: C. mouth; deliver. Verse 17 reads “… all the Gentiles might hear; and I was delivered out of the mouth of the lion.”; verse 18 reads “And the Lord will deliver me from every evil work, and will preserve me for his heavenly Kingdom; to whom be the glory forever and ever. …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Titus
Titus 1
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… to Jewish fables and commandments of men who turn away from the ______.” (1:14); “They ______ that they know God, but by their works they deny him, being abominable, disobedient, and unfit for any good work.” (1:16, WEB) Hard
Answer: truth; profess. The first passage reads “… to Jewish fables and commandments of men who turn away from the truth.”; the second reads “They profess that they know God, but by their works they deny him, being abominable, disobedient, and unfit for any good work.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… men who overthrow whole houses, teaching things which they ought not, for ______ gain's sake.” (1:11); “This ______ is true. For this cause, reprove them sharply, that they may be sound in the faith,” (1:13, WEB) Hard
A) dishonest; counsel B) dishonest; testimony C) testimony; dishonest D) faith; testimony
Answer: B. dishonest; testimony. Verse 11 reads “… men who overthrow whole houses, teaching things which they ought not, for dishonest gain's sake.”; verse 13 reads “This testimony is true. For this cause, reprove them sharply, that they may be sound in the faith,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… servant of God, and an apostle of Jesus Christ, according to the ______ of God's chosen ones, and the knowledge of the truth which is according …” (1:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: faith. The verse reads, “… servant of God, and an apostle of Jesus Christ, according to the faith of God's chosen ones, and the knowledge of the truth which is according …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… as God's steward; not self-pleasing, not easily angered, not given to ______, not ______, not greedy for dishonest gain;” (1:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: wine; violent. The wording is “… as God's steward; not self-pleasing, not easily angered, not given to wine, not violent, not greedy for dishonest gain;” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “I left you in ______ for this reason, that you would set in order the things that were lacking, and appoint elders in every city, as …”? (1:5, WEB) Easy
A) Joppa B) Nazareth C) Crete D) Sychar
Answer: C. Crete. Titus 1:5 uses Crete: “I left you in Crete for this reason, that you would set in order the things that were lacking, and appoint elders in every city, as …”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “holding to the faithful word which is according to the teaching, that he may be able to exhort in the sound doctrine, and to convict those …” (1:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “holding to the faithful word which is according to the teaching, that he may be able to exhort in the sound doctrine, and to convict those …”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “to Titus, my true child according to a common faith: ______, mercy, and peace from God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ our Savior.” (1:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: Grace. The cited words are “to Titus, my true child according to a common faith: Grace, mercy, and peace from God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ our Savior.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “but given to hospitality, as a lover of good, sober-minded, fair, ______, self-controlled;”? (1:8, WEB) Medium
A) innocent B) holy C) poor D) unclean
Answer: B. holy. The passage specifies holy: “but given to hospitality, as a lover of good, sober-minded, fair, holy, self-controlled;”
Titus 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… women likewise be reverent in behavior, not slanderers nor enslaved to much ______, teachers of that which is good;” (2:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: wine. The cited words are “… women likewise be reverent in behavior, not slanderers nor enslaved to much wine, teachers of that which is good;”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “who gave himself for us, that he might redeem us from all ______, and purify for himself a people for his own possession, zealous for good …” (2:14); “Say these things and ______ and reprove with all authority. Let no man despise you.” (2:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: iniquity; exhort. The first passage reads “who gave himself for us, that he might redeem us from all iniquity, and purify for himself a people for his own possession, zealous for good …”; the second reads “Say these things and exhort and reprove with all authority. Let no man despise you.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “and soundness of speech that ______ be condemned; that he who opposes you may be ashamed, having no evil thing to say about us.”? (2:8, WEB) Medium
A) appearing B) sound C) intent D) can't
Answer: D. can't. The passage specifies can't: “and soundness of speech that can't be condemned; that he who opposes you may be ashamed, having no evil thing to say about us.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “instructing us to the ______ that, denying ungodliness and worldly lusts, we would live soberly, righteously, and godly in this present world;” (2:12); “looking for the blessed hope and appearing of the ______ of our great God and Savior, Jesus Christ;” (2:13, WEB) Hard
A) faith; glory B) intent; glory C) glory; intent D) intent; kindness
Answer: B. intent; glory. Verse 12 reads “instructing us to the intent that, denying ungodliness and worldly lusts, we would live soberly, righteously, and godly in this present world;”; verse 13 reads “looking for the blessed hope and appearing of the glory of our great God and Savior, Jesus Christ;”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Exhort servants to be in subjection to their own masters, and to be well-pleasing in all things; not contradicting;” (2:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Exhort servants to be in subjection to their own masters, and to be well-pleasing in all things; not contradicting;”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “in all things showing yourself an example of good ______; in your teaching showing integrity, ______, incorruptibility,” (2:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: works; seriousness. The wording is “in all things showing yourself an example of good works; in your teaching showing integrity, seriousness, incorruptibility,” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “that older men should be temperate, sensible, sober-minded, sound in faith, in ______, and in patience:” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: love. The verse reads, “that older men should be temperate, sensible, sober-minded, sound in faith, in love, and in patience:”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… in subjection to their own husbands, that God's word may not be ______.”? (2:5, WEB) Easy
A) appeared B) condemned C) blessed D) blasphemed
Answer: D. blasphemed. Titus 2:5 uses blasphemed: “… in subjection to their own husbands, that God's word may not be blasphemed.”
Titus 3
Q1. Multiple choice: How are mercy and good works held together? (3:3-8) Hard
A) Mercy means good works can have no place afterward. B) God saves by His mercy rather than our prior righteous deeds, and believers are urged to devote themselves to good works. C) Renewal by the Spirit is absent from the passage. D) Prior good deeds force God to show mercy.
Answer: B. God saves by His mercy rather than our prior righteous deeds, and believers are urged to devote themselves to good works. The sequence resists both earning salvation and treating grace as indifference to conduct. Christian traditions discuss the washing language, but the passage explicitly names mercy and renewal by the Spirit.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… of no one, not to be contentious, to be gentle, showing all ______ toward all men.” (3:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: humility. The verse reads, “… of no one, not to be contentious, to be gentle, showing all humility toward all men.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “This saying is ______, and concerning these things I desire that you affirm confidently, so that those who have believed God may be careful to maintain …”? (3:8, WEB) Medium
A) rich B) faithful C) obedient D) proud
Answer: B. faithful. The passage specifies faithful: “This saying is faithful, and concerning these things I desire that you affirm confidently, so that those who have believed God may be careful to maintain …”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “knowing that such a one is perverted, and ______, being self-condemned.” (3:11); “When I send Artemas to you, or ______, be diligent to come to me to Nicopolis, for I have determined to winter there.” (3:12, WEB) Hard
A) deceit; Tychicus B) Tychicus; sins C) sins; Tychicus D) sins; Andrew
Answer: C. sins; Tychicus. Verse 11 reads “knowing that such a one is perverted, and sins, being self-condemned.”; verse 12 reads “When I send Artemas to you, or Tychicus, be diligent to come to me to Nicopolis, for I have determined to winter there.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “but shun foolish questionings, genealogies, strife, and disputes about the testimonies; for they are unprofitable and vain.” (3:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “but shun foolish questionings, genealogies, strife, and disputes about the law; for they are unprofitable and vain.” It says law, not testimonies.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “that, being justified by his ______, we might be made heirs according to the hope of eternal ______.” (3:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: grace; life. The wording is “that, being justified by his grace, we might be made heirs according to the hope of eternal life.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… by works of righteousness, which we did ourselves, but according to his ______, he saved us, through the washing of regeneration and renewing by the Holy …”? (3:5, WEB) Easy
A) mercy B) glory C) peace D) grief
Answer: A. mercy. Titus 3:5 uses mercy: “… by works of righteousness, which we did ourselves, but according to his mercy, he saved us, through the washing of regeneration and renewing by the Holy …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For we were also once foolish, disobedient, deceived, ______ various lusts and pleasures, living in malice and envy, hateful, and hating one another.” (3:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: serving. The cited words are “For we were also once foolish, disobedient, deceived, serving various lusts and pleasures, living in malice and envy, hateful, and hating one another.”
Philemon
Philemon 1
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Paul, a prisoner of Christ Jesus, and Timothy our brother, to ______, our beloved fellow worker,” (1:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Philemon. The verse reads, “Paul, a prisoner of Christ Jesus, and Timothy our brother, to Philemon, our beloved fellow worker,”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… on your behalf he might serve me in my chains for the ______.”? (1:13, WEB) Medium
A) while B) Gospel C) grace D) Philemon
Answer: B. Gospel. The passage specifies Gospel: “… on your behalf he might serve me in my chains for the Gospel.”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “no prepare as a slave, but more than a slave, a beloved brother, especially to me, but how much rather to you, both in the flesh …” (1:16, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “no longer as a slave, but more than a slave, a beloved brother, especially to me, but how much rather to you, both in the flesh …” It says longer, not prepare.
Q4. Multiple choice: How does Paul's appeal concerning Onesimus challenge an ordinary master-slave relationship? (1:8-21) Hard
A) Onesimus is described as having no place in Christian fellowship. B) He asks Philemon to receive Onesimus as a beloved brother and as he would receive Paul. C) Paul asks that brotherhood make no difference to treatment. D) The letter commands Christians in every era to own slaves.
Answer: B. He asks Philemon to receive Onesimus as a beloved brother and as he would receive Paul. The letter addresses an actual ancient slavery situation. Its appeal to brotherhood and voluntary love should not be turned into an endorsement of owning people or a complete modern legal policy.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “______, my fellow prisoner in Christ Jesus, greets you,” (1:23); “The ______ of our Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit. Amen.” (1:25, WEB) Hard
Answer: Epaphras; grace. The first passage reads “Epaphras, my fellow prisoner in Christ Jesus, greets you,”; the second reads “The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit. Amen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “For we have much ______ and comfort in your love, because the hearts of the saints have been refreshed through you, brother.”? (1:7, WEB) Easy
A) mercy B) fear C) love D) joy
Answer: D. joy. Philemon 1:7 uses joy: “For we have much joy and comfort in your love, because the hearts of the saints have been refreshed through you, brother.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “hearing of your love, and of the ______ which you have toward the Lord Jesus, and toward all the saints;” (1:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: faith. The cited words are “hearing of your love, and of the faith which you have toward the Lord Jesus, and toward all the saints;”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… I rather beg, being such a one as ______, the aged, but also a prisoner of Jesus ______.” (1:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: Paul; Christ. The wording is “… I rather beg, being such a one as Paul, the aged, but also a prisoner of Jesus Christ.” The missing terms belong in that order.
